% QUESTION 195
% Number: Question 195
% Type: MCQ | Marks: 1 Mark
% Tags: GATE EC 2001, Antennas
% Text HTML: <div class="question_text" data-value="1">In a uniform linear array, four isotropic radiating elements are spaced [latex]\frac{\lambda }{4}[/latex] apart.
The progressive phase shift between the elements required for forming the main
beam at [latex]60^{\circ}[/latex] off the end-fire is:</div>
% Option A (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]- \pi[/latex] radians</div>
% Option B (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]-\frac{\pi }{2} [/latex] radians</div>
% Option C (Correct=True): <div class="option_data">[latex]-\frac{\pi }{4} [/latex] radians</div>
% Option D (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]-\frac{\pi }{8} [/latex] radians</div>
% Explanation HTML: <div class="mtq_explanation-text">
[latex]\theta_{\max }=60^{\circ}[/latex] from end fire means 60^{\circ} from array axis.\\ Condition for maximum, [latex]\psi \rightarrow 0 [/latex]<br/>[latex] \begin{array}{l} E_{T}=E_{O} \frac{\sin \left(N_{\psi} / 2\right)}{\sin (\psi / 2)} \\ E_{T}=N E_{0}=E_{\max } \\ \psi=\alpha+\beta d \cos 60^{\circ}=0 \\ \alpha+\frac{2 \pi}{\lambda} \cdot \frac{\lambda}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{2}=0 \\ \alpha=-\frac{\pi}{4} \end{array}[/latex]</div>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

% QUESTION 196
% Number: Question 196
% Type: MCQ | Marks: 1 Mark
% Tags: GATE EC 2001, Antennas
% Text HTML: <div class="question_text" data-value="1">A medium wave radio transmitter operating at a wavelength of 492 m has a
tower antenna of height 124m. What is the radiation resistance of the antenna?</div>
% Option A (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]25\Omega[/latex]</div>
% Option B (Correct=True): <div class="option_data">[latex]36.5 \Omega[/latex]</div>
% Option C (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]50\Omega[/latex]</div>
% Option D (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]73 \Omega[/latex]</div>
% Explanation HTML: <div class="mtq_explanation-text">
[latex]\frac{\lambda}{4}=\frac{492}{4}=123 \simeq L [/latex]<br/>[latex] \therefore \quad [/latex]It is a quarter wave monopole antenna.<br/> So, [latex]\quad R_{a}=36.5 \Omega[/latex]</div>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

% QUESTION 197
% Number: Question 197
% Type: MCQ | Marks: 1 Mark
% Tags: GATE EC 2001, Transmission Lines
% Text HTML: <div class="question_text" data-value="1">A uniform plane electromagnetic wave incident normally on a plane surface of a
dielectric material is reflected with a VSWR of 3. What is the percentage of
incident power that is reflected?</div>
% Option A (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">10%</div>
% Option B (Correct=True): <div class="option_data">25%</div>
% Option C (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">50%</div>
% Option D (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">75%</div>
% Explanation HTML: <div class="mtq_explanation-text">
[latex]\begin{aligned} s&amp;=\frac{1+|\Gamma|}{1-|\Gamma|} \\ \Rightarrow 3&amp;=\frac{1+|\Gamma|}{1-|\Gamma|} \\ |\Gamma|&amp;=0.5 \\ \frac{P_{r}}{P_{i}}&amp;=|\Gamma|^{2}=0.25 \end{aligned}[/latex]<br/>[latex] \therefore \quad 25 \% [/latex] of incident power is reflected.</div>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

% QUESTION 198
% Number: Question 198
% Type: MCQ | Marks: 1 Mark
% Tags: GATE EC 2001, Uniform Plane Waves
% Text HTML: <div class="question_text" data-value="1">A material has conductivity of [latex]10^{-2}[/latex]
mho/m and a relative permittivity of 4. The
frequency at which the conduction current in the medium is equal to the
displacement current is</div>
% Option A (Correct=True): <div class="option_data">45 MHz</div>
% Option B (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">90 MHz</div>
% Option C (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">450 MHz</div>
% Option D (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">900 MHz</div>
% Explanation HTML: <div class="mtq_explanation-text">
[latex]\begin{aligned} |\sigma E| &amp;=|j \omega \in E| \\ \sigma &amp;=2 \pi f \epsilon_{0} \epsilon_{r} \\ \Rightarrow \quad f &amp;=\frac{\sigma}{2 \pi \times \epsilon_{0} \epsilon_{r}} \\ &amp;=\frac{9 \times 10^{9} \times 2 \times 10^{-2}}{4} \\ f &amp;=45 \times 10^{6}=45 \mathrm{MHz} \end{aligned}[/latex]</div>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

% QUESTION 202
% Number: Question 202
% Type: MCQ | Marks: 1 Mark
% Tags: GATE EC 2001, Transmission Lines
% Text HTML: <div class="question_text" data-value="1">A transmission line is distortion-less if</div>
% Option A (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">[latex]RL=\frac{1}{GC}[/latex]</div>
% Option B (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">RL=GC</div>
% Option C (Correct=True): <div class="option_data">LG=RC</div>
% Option D (Correct=False): <div class="option_data">RG=LC</div>
% Explanation HTML: <div class="mtq_explanation-text"></div>

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
